## From the manufacturer

![Image 34: gearwrench, automotive, wrench, tools, ratchet, socket, professional, crescent, mechanic, pliers](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

![Image 36: gearwrench, automotive, wrench, tools, ratchet, socket, professional, crescent, mechanic, pliers](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

GEARWRENCH was launched in 1996 with the first professional ratcheting wrench. Since then, our brand has become synonymous with ratcheting wrenches designed for professional automotive technicians and industrial users. By 2002, over 100 million wrenches had been sold and the brand had earned a name for producing the highest quality tools at a great value. Since its launch, the ratcheting wrench range has developed into a line of over 20 different styles and 600 SKUs, solving the access problems of millions of users.

In 2006, GEARWRENCH introduced the broadest line expansion in its history with the addition of ratchets, sockets, screwdrivers, pliers, and torque wrenches. Since then, we have continued to expand our listing with bit sockets, impact sockets, and pry bars to become a full line mechanics hand tool supplier.

We are a premier mechanics hand tool brand, offering products that are designed and manufactured to meet the requirements of professionals making a living with their tools. We understand the problems they face every day and provide tools that increase productivity through SPEED, STRENGTH, and ACCESS. GEARWRENCH tools are tough and innovative.

## Product Description

Since our beginning, GEARWRENCH automotive specialty tools have been driven by innovation. From our first specialty tool, the Model-T valve spring lifter, to our latest ratcheting puller, we’ve built our reputation with tools that make tough jobs easy. Most of our products have been upgraded with new improved features like dual material handles designed for a more comfortable grip or dual dipped handles on pliers providing more cushion for your hands. GEARWRENCH automotive specialty tools are proudly made with the finest components under the most stringent manufacturing guidelines. Made for everyday use – guaranteed for life. Better tools mean easier, more efficient work so we constantly evaluate automotive engineering trends allowing us to consistently improve and enhance our tools to better fit professional hands. That’s why they are the first choice for thousands of automotive technicians worldwide.

* * *

#### WARNING:

CHOKING HAZARD -- Small parts. Not for children under 3 yrs.

[How customer reviews and ratings work](javascript:void(0))

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

[Learn more how customers reviews work on Amazon](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_omni_lm_btn?nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)

### Customers say

Customers find the wrench works better than metal filter removers, particularly in tight spots, and consider it the best tool they've owned. It effectively removes stuck filters with ease and is considered a lifesaver. The durability receives mixed feedback, with some saying it's durable while others report it breaking right away. The fit is generally good, though one customer notes the hole is slightly too small for a 1/2" drive to fit properly.

## Product Description

 Since our beginning, GEARWRENCH automotive specialty tools have been driven by innovation. From our first specialty tool, the Model-T valve spring lifter, to our latest ratcheting puller, we’ve built our reputation with tools that make tough jobs easy. Most of our products have been upgraded with new improved features like dual material handles designed for a more comfortable grip or dual dipped handles on pliers providing more cushion for your hands. GEARWRENCH automotive specialty tools are proudly made with the finest components under the most stringent manufacturing guidelines. Made for everyday use – guaranteed for life. Better tools mean easier, more efficient work so we constantly evaluate automotive engineering trends allowing us to consistently improve and enhance our tools to better fit professional hands. That’s why they are the first choice for thousands of automotive technicians worldwide.
